- **save-context-for-compact**: Do not invoke this on your own. Use it only when the user explicitly invokes `save-context-for-compact` by name, asks to run it, or as the first step of the explicitly invoked wrapup skill. Never infer it from mentions of saving context, checkpointing, persisting knowledge, compacting, or wrapping up.
